ADDED `GET /api/deepgram-token` (mints JWT + Settings) + `POST /api/agent/vocab` (vocab save callback)\n149\t- `public/chat.js`: `openVoiceAgent()` now fetches token then connects direct to Deepgram. `handleAgentMsg` handles `Welcome` (sends Settings) + `FunctionCallRequest` (browser-side vocab save)\n150\t\n151\t**Run 21-22 files changed:**\n152\t- `src/index.js`: Added `/api/agent` WebSocket proxy handler + `buildAgentPrompt()` helper\n153\t- `public/chat.js`: Added Voice Agent mode (openVoiceAgent, handleAgentMsg, startAgentListening, stopAgentListening, playAgentChunk)\n154\t- REST pipeline kept as fallback when Voice Agent WS unavailable\n155\t\n156\t**DeepSeek config in Voice Agent:**\n157\t\n158\t```json\n159\t{\n160\t  \"think\": {\n161\t    \"provider\": { \"type\": \"open_ai\", \"model\": \"deepseek-chat\", \"temperature\": 0.8 },\n162\t    \"endpoint\": { \"url\": \"https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions\" }\n163\t  }\n164\t}\n165\t```\n166\t\n167\t**Expected latency:** < 2s vs ~8s with 3-step REST pipeline.\n168\t\n169\t### Portal Mobile Fixes (all 3 portals)\n170\t\n171\t- **Streak badge dismiss button** — Added `×` close button (`.sb-dismiss`) to badge HTML. Streak badge can now be dismissed on mobile.\n172\t- **Hamburger backdrop** — Replaced broken `document.addEventListener('click',...)` click-outside approach with `#navBackdrop` transparent overlay at `z-index:8999`. Tapping backdrop calls `closeNav()`. IDs: `navToggle` (button), `navLinks` (menu), `navBackdrop` (overlay). Applied to all 3 portals.\n173\t\n174\t---\n175\t\n176\t## OBI Voice + Chat Fixes — 2026-05-29\n177\t\n178\t### Root Cause: Serial context fetches killed latency\n179\t\n180\t`handleChat()` in `index.js` had **7 sequential `await` calls** before the DeepSeek call:\n181\tLedger DO → Session DO → Weakness DO → Pet DO → `buildStructuredContext()` (4 more DOs) → Soul logger HTTP → KV reads.\n182\tEach could be 200-500ms. Total: up to 4-8 seconds before the LLM even started.\n183\t\n184\t**Fix:** Collapsed all 7 into a single `Promise.allSettled([...])` round — all fire simultaneously. Time = slowest single fetch, not the sum.\n185\t\n186\tAlso: `rosterStub.fetch()` and `updateStudentMemory()` were both `await`ed AFTER DeepSeek returned. Made fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})`).\n187\t\n188\t### Root Cause: Bilingual mode silently dropped\n189\t\n190\t`capContextInjection()` has a 1200-char hard cap. `vietnameseContext` was passed as the 8th argument — ledger/session/weakness filled the budget first, Vietnamese instruction was silently cut every time.\n191\t\n192\t**Fix:** `vietnameseContext` and `teachingFocusContext` now passed FIRST in `capContextInjection` — guaranteed never truncated.\n193\t\n194\t### Root Cause: teachingFocus never reached the server\n195\t\n196\t`handleChat()` only read `lang:<id>` from KV, never the student profile. `teachingFocus` (\"Speak VERY slowly, short sentences...\") was only available client-side, but the server-side prompt REPLACES the client's system prompt.\n197\t\n198\t**Fix:** `handleChat()` now reads `student_profile_<id>` from KV in parallel with `lang:<id>`, extracts `teachingFocus`, injects as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block.\n199\t\n200\t### Root Cause: isVague() matched \"anything\" as substring\n201\t\n202\tPattern `/anything|something|everything/` matched inside any sentence containing those words. Student saying \"Anything about health interests me\" triggered the \"Come on! Say something!\" scripted fallback instead of the LLM.\n203\t\n204\t**Fix:** Only match as standalone response: `/^(whatever|anything|something|everything)\\.?\\s*$/`\n205\t\n206\t### Deepgram Voice Agent — Text routing\n207\t\n208\tWhen student types a message (fallback path), it now routes through the open Deepgram Voice Agent WS as `{type: \"TextInput\", text}` instead of hitting `/api/chat` → DeepSeek. Deepgram's in-house LLM responds via `ConversationText` event in ~200-500ms.\n209\t\n210\t- `agentTextResolve` promise pattern: `reactLLM()` sets resolver, `handleAgentMsg` resolves it on assistant `ConversationText`\n211\t- 6-second timeout → falls back to REST if agent doesn't respond\n212\t\n213\t### Voice-First UI: magic-chat.html\n214\t\n215\tApp is a speaking trainer — text input removed from visible UI.\n216\t- `#msg` input and `#sendBtn` hidden via CSS (`display: none`) — kept in DOM so JS refs don't break\n217\t- `#mic` button made large (72×72px), centered, prominent\n218\t- `#micStatus` text element added below mic button — shows: \"Tap to start\" / \"Connecting…\" / \"Listening…\" / \"Processing…\"\n219\t- `#mic.mic-active` pulsing purple animation (existing)\n220\t\n221\t### Auto-mic: gesture-time stream acquisition\n222\t\n223\tBrowser security requires `getUserMedia()` to be called inside a user gesture event. Calling it from a WebSocket `onmessage` handler silently fails even if the user tapped earlier.\n224\t\n225\t**Fix:** On first tap/click/keydown anywhere on the page, `getUserMedia()` is called immediately (inside gesture context). Stream stored in `pendingMicStream`. When `SettingsApplied` fires from Deepgram, the stored stream is connected directly — no second permission request needed.\n226\t\n227\tVoice transcriptions now appear as student chat bubbles (previously dumped into `input.value`).\n228\t\n229\t### Root Cause: Wrong Deepgram Voice Agent URL (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n230\t\n231\tWe were connecting to `https://agent.deepgram.com/` — **wrong URL**. Correct: `https://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse`. This caused slow/failed connections. Always use the full path.\n232\t\n233\t### Root Cause: Wrong text injection event name (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n234\t\n235\tWe sent `{type: \"TextInput\", text: \"...\"}` — **does not exist** in Deepgram API. Correct: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}`. Verified from official docs.\n236\t\n237\t### Root Cause: BYO DeepSeek LLM caused unnecessary external hop\n238\t\n239\tWe used `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `endpoint.url: deepseek.com` + `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`. This routes through an external API (China-hosted, variable latency).\n240\t\n241\t**Fix**: Deepgram manages OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, NVIDIA models natively — **no API key needed**. Just set `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` with `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` and NO `endpoint` field. Deepgram handles the LLM call on their own infrastructure. Remove all external LLM endpoints from the Voice Agent settings.\n242\t\n243\t### Root Cause: openVoiceAgent() called too late in boot (15-second startup)\n244\t\n245\t`openVoiceAgent()` was called at the END of boot() after 5 serial awaits. Voice Agent connection didn't start until 5+ seconds into page load.\n246\t\n247\t**Fix**: Call `openVoiceAgent()` immediately after `urlStudentId` is extracted (first thing in boot). Also parallelized all 4 boot API calls (state + memory + vocab + hint) with `Promise.allSettled`.\n248\t\n249\t### Root Cause: /api/agent blocked 101 response on Deepgram connection\n250\t\n251\tThe CF Worker was `await fetch(\"agent.deepgram.com\")` BEFORE returning the 101 WebSocket upgrade. Browser was stuck waiting for the Deepgram TCP handshake.\n252\t\n253\t**Fix**: Return 101 to browser immediately, connect to Deepgram via `ctx.waitUntil()` in background. Queue any early browser messages until Deepgram is ready.\n254\t\n255\t### Deepgram Voice Agent — Confirmed Correct Spec (READ BEFORE TOUCHING AGENT CODE)\n256\t\n257\t- **WS URL**: `wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse` (NOT just `/`)\n258\t- **Auth**: `Authorization: Token {DEEPGRAM_API_KEY}` header on the WS upgrade fetch\n259\t- **Settings**: sent after receiving `Welcome` event from server\n260\t- **User text injection**: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}` (NOT TextInput)\n261\t- **Agent speech injection**: `{type: \"InjectAgentMessage\", content: \"...\"}`\n262\t- **Managed LLMs (no key)**: `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` with NO `endpoint` — Deepgram pays for it\n263\t- **Managed LLMs available**: OpenAI, Anthropic (`claude-4-5-haiku-latest`), Google (`gemini-2.5-flash`), NVIDIA\n264\t- **BYO LLMs require key**: Groq, AWS Bedrock — need your own endpoint + credentials\n265\t- **STT model**: `nova-3` (or `flux-general-en` for newer)\n266\t- **TTS model**: `aura-2-asteria-en`\n267\t- **Client sends audio**: binary PCM16 frames directly over WS\n268\t- **Server events**: `Welcome`, `SettingsApplied`, `ConversationText` (role: user|assistant), `AgentAudioDone`, `UserStartedSpeaking`, binary MP3 chunks\n269\t\n270\t### Deploy History (2026-05-29)\n271\t| Version ID | What changed choppy/stuttering, especially on mobile devices. Root cause: each MP3 chunk from Deepgram created a separate `Audio` element. The `onended → next play()` transition introduced 20-80ms silence gaps per chunk. On budget Android phones with slow `Audio.play()` latency (150-300ms), this compounded into audible stuttering.\n652\t\n653\t### Fix Applied\n654\t**Gapless Playback v2** (`chat.js`):\n655\t1. **Chunk Concatenation:** All queued MP3 chunks merged into single `Uint8Array` before creating `Audio` element. Eliminates N-1 inter-chunk gaps entirely.\n656\t2. **Resource Cleanup:** Barge-in (`UserStartedSpeaking`) and disconnect (`onAgentDrop`) handlers explicitly revoke concatenated blob URLs. No memory leaks from orphaned object URLs.\n657\t3. **Pre-buffer Gate REMOVED:** Initial v2 had a \"wait for ≥2 chunks\" gate that caused OBI to be cut off entirely on multi-message responses. Gate removed — concatenation alone solves choppiness without holding back audio.\n658\t\n659\t### What Was NOT Changed\n660\t- Accumulator threshold stays at 2KB (`CHUNK_FLUSH_BYTES = 2048`)\n661\t- Queue cap stays at 30 entries\n662\t- Audio format remains MP3 (Deepgram TTS output)\n663\t- No MSE/Web Audio API PCM scheduling (would require full rewrite)\n664\t\n665\t### Testing\n666\t- Confirmed smooth playback on high-end laptop (was already choppy before fix)\n667\t- Multi-message responses no longer cut off\n668\t- Barge-in still works correctly (audio stops immediately)\n669\t- Test sandbox: https://net-pet-ai.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev/magic-test\n670\t\n671\t### Hardening v3 (2026-05-31 post-deploy)\n672\tAdditional defensive guards added after initial gapless fix:\n673\t\n674\t1. **Stuck Playback Watchdog:** If `onended` never fires (corrupted MP3 frame, browser bug), a timeout watchdog forces cleanup and restarts playback. Duration estimated from blob size / 16KB/s + 2s buffer.\n675\t2. **Queue-Aware Guard:** Previous guard blocked ALL playback while audio was \"playing.\" New guard checks queue state — if chunks arrive but audio is stuck, forces cleanup and restart instead of silently dropping new audio.\n676\t3. **Comment Accuracy:** Fixed silence timer comment (was \"4s\", actual is \"6s\"). Removed stale pre-buffer gate reference.\n677\t\n678\t### Remaining Known Issues\n679\t- iOS mic reconnect after drop still broken (Open Loop #4 from COLD-START-BOOT)\n680\t- Raw Deepgram API key exposed to browser (Edge Case #3 from Run 30 audit)\n681\t- Silence nudge race condition between AgentAudioDone + playAgentChunk drain (Edge Case #2)\n682\t- Deepgram docs URL CHANGED: canonical hub is now `https://developers.deepgram.com/home` (NOT `/docs`). Full AI-readable docs at `https://developers.deepgram.com/llms-full.txt`. MCP server at `https://developers.deepgram.com/_mcp/server`. Old `/docs/voice-agents` paths return 404. Updated 2026-05-31.\n683\t\n684\t---\n685\t\n686\t## RUN 30 SESSION COMPLETE — 2026-05-31 FULL CHANGELOG\n687\t\n688\t### Overview\n689\tRun 30 was a comprehensive hardening + feature sprint covering: audio pipeline fix, full-stack error logging, 3-mode session system, landing page UI, test portal, and edge case remediation. 12 deployments total.\n690\t\n691\t---\n692\t\n693\t### 1. Applied\n\n1. **Boot endpoint fixed** — `chat.js` was calling `/api/netpet/state` (PetDO only, no KV profile). Fixed to `/api/student/state` which returns full KV profile including `teachingFocus`, `primaryBlock`, `lang`, `breakthrough`.\n2. **buildSystemPrompt enriched** — Now injects `teachingFocus` as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block + `lang === 'vi'` triggers BILINGUAL MODE (Vietnamese translations after every sentence).\n3. **KV profile fields mapped** — Boot now maps `teachingFocus`, `primaryBlock`, `breakthrough`, `sessionNotes`, `lang`, `streak` from `/api/student/state` into the client-side profile.\n\n### Voice Agent — Architecture (UPDATED Run 23)\n\n**Architecture (Run 23 — CURRENT):**\n```\nBrowser → GET /api/deepgram-token (CF Worker builds Settings + returns API key)\nBrowser → wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse (DIRECT — no CF proxy in audio path)\n```\n\n**DEPRECATED (Run 21-22 — BROKEN):** Browser PCM16 → `/api/agent` WS proxy → Deepgram\nReason removed: CF regular Workers cannot sustain long-lived WS sessions.\nctx.waitUntil was terminated by CF runtime every ~1-2s → infinite reconnect loop.\n\n**Files changed (Run 23):**\n- `src/index.js`: REMOVED `/api/agent` proxy. ADDED `GET /api/deepgram-token` (mints JWT + Settings) + `POST /api/agent/vocab` (vocab save callback)\n- `public/chat.js`: `openVoiceAgent()` now fetches token then connects direct to Deepgram. `handleAgentMsg` handles `Welcome` (sends Settings) + `FunctionCallRequest` (browser-side vocab save)\n\n**Run 21-22 files changed:**\n- `src/index.js`: Added `/api/agent` WebSocket proxy handler + `buildAgentPrompt()` helper\n- `public/chat.js`: Added Voice Agent mode (openVoiceAgent, handleAgentMsg, startAgentListening, stopAgentListening, playAgentChunk)\n- REST pipeline kept as fallback when Voice Agent WS unavailable\n\n**DeepSeek config in Voice Agent:**\n\n```json\n{\n  \"think\": {\n    \"provider\": { \"type\": \"open_ai\", \"model\": \"deepseek-chat\", \"temperature\": 0.8 },\n    \"endpoint\": { \"url\": \"https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions\" }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Expected latency:** < 2s vs ~8s with 3-step REST pipeline.\n\n### Portal Mobile Fixes (all 3 portals)\n\n- **Streak badge dismiss button** — Added `×` close button (`.sb-dismiss`) to badge HTML. Streak badge can now be dismissed on mobile.\n- **Hamburger backdrop** — Replaced broken `document.addEventListener('click',...)` click-outside approach with `#navBackdrop` transparent overlay at `z-index:8999`. Tapping backdrop calls `closeNav()`. IDs: `navToggle` (button), `navLinks` (menu), `navBackdrop` (overlay). Applied to all 3 portals.\n\n---\n\n## OBI Voice + Chat Fixes — 2026-05-29\n\n### Root Cause: Serial context fetches killed latency\n\n`handleChat()` in `index.js` had **7 sequential `await` calls** before the DeepSeek call:\nLedger DO → Session DO → Weakness DO → Pet DO → `buildStructuredContext()` (4 more DOs) → Soul logger HTTP → KV reads.\nEach could be 200-500ms. Total: up to 4-8 seconds before the LLM even started.\n\n**Fix:** Collapsed all 7 into a single `Promise.allSettled([...])` round — all fire simultaneously. Time = slowest single fetch, not the sum.\n\nAlso: `rosterStub.fetch()` and `updateStudentMemory()` were both `await`ed AFTER DeepSeek returned. Made fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})`).\n\n### Root Cause: Bilingual mode silently dropped\n\n`capContextInjection()` has a 1200-char hard cap. `vietnameseContext` was passed as the 8th argument — ledger/session/weakness filled the budget first, Vietnamese instruction was silently cut every time.\n\n**Fix:** `vietnameseContext` and `teachingFocusContext` now passed FIRST in `capContextInjection` — guaranteed never truncated.\n\n### Root Cause: teachingFocus never reached the server\n\n`handleChat()` only read `lang:<id>` from KV, never the student profile. `teachingFocus` (\"Speak VERY slowly, short sentences...\") was only available client-side, but the server-side prompt REPLACES the client's system prompt.\n\n**Fix:** `handleChat()` now reads `student_profile_<id>` from KV in parallel with `lang:<id>`, extracts `teachingFocus`, injects as `TEACHER INSTRUCTION` block.\n\n### Root Cause: isVague() matched \"anything\" as substring\n\nPattern `/anything|something|everything/` matched inside any sentence containing those words. Student saying \"Anything about health interests me\" triggered the \"Come on! Say something!\" scripted fallback instead of the LLM.\n\n**Fix:** Only match as standalone response: `/^(whatever|anything|something|everything)\\.?\\s*$/`\n\n### Deepgram Voice Agent — Text routing\n\nWhen student types a message (fallback path), it now routes through the open Deepgram Voice Agent WS as `{type: \"TextInput\", text}` instead of hitting `/api/chat` → DeepSeek. Deepgram's in-house LLM responds via `ConversationText` event in ~200-500ms.\n\n- `agentTextResolve` promise pattern: `reactLLM()` sets resolver, `handleAgentMsg` resolves it on assistant `ConversationText`\n- 6-second timeout → falls back to REST if agent doesn't respond\n\n### Voice-First UI: magic-chat.html\n\nApp is a speaking trainer — text input removed from visible UI.\n- `#msg` input and `#sendBtn` hidden via CSS (`display: none`) — kept in DOM so JS refs don't break\n- `#mic` button made large (72×72px), centered, prominent\n- `#micStatus` text element added below mic button — shows: \"Tap to start\" / \"Connecting…\" / \"Listening…\" / \"Processing…\"\n- `#mic.mic-active` pulsing purple animation (existing)\n\n### Auto-mic: gesture-time stream acquisition\n\nBrowser security requires `getUserMedia()` to be called inside a user gesture event. Calling it from a WebSocket `onmessage` handler silently fails even if the user tapped earlier.\n\n**Fix:** On first tap/click/keydown anywhere on the page, `getUserMedia()` is called immediately (inside gesture context). Stream stored in `pendingMicStream`. When `SettingsApplied` fires from Deepgram, the stored stream is connected directly — no second permission request needed.\n\nVoice transcriptions now appear as student chat bubbles (previously dumped into `input.value`).\n\n### Root Cause: Wrong Deepgram Voice Agent URL (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n\nWe were connecting to `https://agent.deepgram.com/` — **wrong URL**. Correct: `https://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse`. This caused slow/failed connections. Always use the full path.\n\n### Root Cause: Wrong text injection event name (SESSION LOOP — STOP REPEATING)\n\nWe sent `{type: \"TextInput\", text: \"...\"}` — **does not exist** in Deepgram API. Correct: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}`. Verified from official docs.\n\n### Root Cause: BYO DeepSeek LLM caused unnecessary external hop\n\nWe used `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `endpoint.url: deepseek.com` + `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`. This routes through an external API (China-hosted, variable latency).\n\n**Fix**: Deepgram manages OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, NVIDIA models natively — **no API key needed**. Just set `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` with `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` and NO `endpoint` field. Deepgram handles the LLM call on their own infrastructure. Remove all external LLM endpoints from the Voice Agent settings.\n\n### Root Cause: openVoiceAgent() called too late in boot (15-second startup)\n\n`openVoiceAgent()` was called at the END of boot() after 5 serial awaits. Voice Agent connection didn't start until 5+ seconds into page load.\n\n**Fix**: Call `openVoiceAgent()` immediately after `urlStudentId` is extracted (first thing in boot). Also parallelized all 4 boot API calls (state + memory + vocab + hint) with `Promise.allSettled`.\n\n### Root Cause: /api/agent blocked 101 response on Deepgram connection\n\nThe CF Worker was `await fetch(\"agent.deepgram.com\")` BEFORE returning the 101 WebSocket upgrade. Browser was stuck waiting for the Deepgram TCP handshake.\n\n**Fix**: Return 101 to browser immediately, connect to Deepgram via `ctx.waitUntil()` in background. Queue any early browser messages until Deepgram is ready.\n\n### Deepgram Voice Agent — Confirmed Correct Spec (READ BEFORE TOUCHING AGENT CODE)\n\n- **WS URL**: `wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse` (NOT just `/`)\n- **Auth**: `Authorization: Token {DEEPGRAM_API_KEY}` header on the WS upgrade fetch\n- **Settings**: sent after receiving `Welcome` event from server\n- **User text injection**: `{type: \"InjectUserMessage\", content: \"...\"}` (NOT TextInput)\n- **Agent speech injection**: `{type: \"InjectAgentMessage\", content: \"...\"}`\n- **Managed LLMs (no key)**: `provider.type: \"open_ai\"` + `model: \"gpt-4o-mini\"` with NO `endpoint` — Deepgram pays for it\n- **Managed LLMs available**: OpenAI, Anthropic (`claude-4-5-haiku-latest`), Google (`gemini-2.5-flash`), NVIDIA\n- **BYO LLMs require key**: Groq, AWS Bedrock — need your own endpoint + credentials\n- **STT model**: `nova-3` (or `flux-general-en` for newer)\n- **TTS model**: `aura-2-asteria-en`\n- **Client sends audio**: binary PCM16 frames directly over WS\n- **Server events**: `Welcome`, `SettingsApplied`, `ConversationText` (role: user|assistant), `AgentAudioDone`, `UserStartedSpeaking`, binary MP3 chunks\n\n### Deploy History (2026-05-29)\n| Version ID | What changed |\n|---|---|\n| `8c34e784` | handleChat bilingual + teachingFocus fix |\n| `d2212f87` | Mic button cycles on/off in reconnect loop. No audio pickup.\n**Symptom:** WS connects → drops immediately → reconnects → drops → infinite loop\n**Diagnosis (wrangler tail + /api/deepgram-test):**\n- DEEPGRAM_API_KEY: VALID — diagnostic confirmed `status:101, hasWebSocket:true`\n- Deepgram project ID: `70543a02-9a08-400b-a814-8b0b01184f7a`\n- CF Worker returns 101 to browser (outcome: ok, wallTime: 3ms)\n- Zero `[AGENT]` console.log output in tail — background task crashes before `dgAgentWs.accept()`\n- Error: \"Workers runtime canceled this request — code hung, would never generate a response\"\n\n**ROOT CAUSE (ARCHITECTURAL):**\nCF regular Workers cannot maintain long-lived WebSocket proxy sessions.\nThe Worker returns 101 and starts `ctx.waitUntil`, but CF terminates the background task\nbefore the Deepgram WS session can be sustained. This is a CF platform limitation —\nregular Workers are not designed for long-lived bidirectional WS proxying.\nDurable Objects are required for that pattern — OR the browser connects directly.\n\n**DECISION: Switch to browser-direct Deepgram connection.**\n```\nOLD (broken): Browser → CF Worker WS proxy → Deepgram\nNEW (correct): Browser → GET /api/agent/token (CF Worker mints temp key + builds Settings)\n               Browser → wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse (direct, no CF in audio path)\n```\nCF Worker role: mint a short-lived Deepgram temp key + return pre-built Settings payload.\nBrowser opens direct WS to Deepgram using the temp key. API key never exposed in JS.\nFunctionCall events (vocab saving) handled by a separate POST endpoint instead of WS interception.\n\n**RUN 23 RESOLUTION — 2026-05-29**\n\nResearch agents confirmed:\n- `functions` placement in `agent.think.functions[]` was CORRECT all along\n- `eot_threshold` only valid on Flux model — removed from nova-3 Settings\n- `/v1/auth/grant` temp token: 403 (key lacks scope) — using raw API key via CF secret instead\n- CF Worker WS proxy pattern is architecturally unsupported for long-lived sessions\n\n**Architecture switch applied (deploy `c05815b7`):**\n- REMOVED: `/api/agent` WS proxy (179 lines — the entire broken pattern)\n- ADDED: `GET /api/deepgram-token?studentId=<id>` — returns `{token, settings}` (JWT from CF secret + pre-built Settings payload with student profile/prompt)\n- ADDED: `POST /api/agent/vocab` — browser calls this when FunctionCallRequest received\n- Browser now connects: `new WebSocket(\"wss://agent.deepgram.com/v1/agent/converse\", [\"token\", jwt])`\n- Settings sent from browser on `Welcome` event with conversation history injected\n- Function calls handled entirely client-side + POST callback\n\n**Token endpoint verified:** token_length=40, nova-3, gpt-4o-mini, aura-2-asteria-en, prompt=2249 chars, save_vocabulary_word function registered\n\n### Open Items After FIX — GAPLESS PLAYBACK v2 (2026-05-31)\n\n### Problem\nVoice agent audio was choppy/stuttering, especially on mobile devices. Root cause: each MP3 chunk from Deepgram created a separate `Audio` element. The `onended → next play()` transition introduced 20-80ms silence gaps per chunk. On budget Android phones with slow `Audio.play()` latency (150-300ms), this compounded into audible stuttering.\n\n### Fix Applied\n**Gapless Playback v2** (`chat.js`):\n1. **Chunk Concatenation:** All queued MP3 chunks merged into single `Uint8Array` before creating `Audio` element. Eliminates N-1 inter-chunk gaps entirely.\n2. **Resource Cleanup:** Barge-in (`UserStartedSpeaking`) and disconnect (`onAgentDrop`) handlers explicitly revoke concatenated blob URLs. No memory leaks from orphaned object URLs.\n3. **Pre-buffer Gate REMOVED:** Initial v2 had a \"wait for ≥2 chunks\" gate that caused OBI to be cut off entirely on multi-message responses. Gate removed — concatenation alone solves choppiness without holding back audio.\n\n### What Was NOT Changed\n- Accumulator threshold stays at 2KB (`CHUNK_FLUSH_BYTES = 2048`)\n- Queue cap stays at 30 entries\n- Audio format remains MP3 (Deepgram TTS output)\n- No MSE/Web Audio API PCM scheduling (would require full rewrite)\n\n### Testing\n- Confirmed smooth playback on high-end laptop (was already choppy before fix)\n- Multi-message responses no longer cut off\n- Barge-in still works correctly (audio stops immediately)\n- Test sandbox: https://net-pet-ai.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev/magic-test\n\n### Hardening v3 (2026-05-31 post-deploy)\nAdditional defensive guards added after initial gapless fix:\n\n1. **Stuck Playback Watchdog:** If `onended` never fires (corrupted MP3 frame, browser bug), a timeout watchdog forces cleanup and restarts playback. Duration estimated from blob size / 16KB/s + 2s buffer.\n2. **Queue-Aware Guard:** Previous guard blocked ALL playback while audio was \"playing.\" New guard checks queue state — if chunks arrive but audio is stuck, forces cleanup and restart instead of silently dropping new audio.\n3. **Comment Accuracy:** Fixed silence timer comment (was \"4s\", actual is \"6s\"). Removed stale pre-buffer gate reference.\n\n### Remaining Known Issues\n- iOS mic reconnect after drop still broken (Open Loop #4 from COLD-START-BOOT)\n- Raw Deepgram API key exposed to browser (Edge Case #3 from Run 30 audit)\n- Silence nudge race condition between AgentAudioDone + playAgentChunk drain (Edge Case #2)\n- Deepgram docs URL CHANGED: canonical hub is now `https://developers.deepgram.com/home` (NOT `/docs`). Full AI-readable docs at `https://developers.deepgram.com/llms-full.txt`. MCP server at `https://developers.deepgram.com/_mcp/server`. Old `/docs/voice-agents` paths return 404. Updated 2026-05-31.\n\n---\n\n## RUN 30 SESSION COMPLETE — 2026-05-31 FULL CHANGELOG\n\n### Overview\nRun 30 was a comprehensive hardening + feature sprint covering: audio pipeline fix, full-stack error logging, 3-mode session system, landing page UI, test portal, and edge case remediation. 12 deployments total.\n\n---\n\n### 1. |\n\n### FLUX ARCHITECTURE FINDINGS (from llms-full.txt)\n\n**Flux = Model-integrated end-of-turn detection.** Replaces VAD-based turn detection with model-native signals:\n- `EndOfTurn` — High confidence user finished → send to LLM\n- `EagerEndOfTurn` — Medium confidence → prepare LLM response early (lower latency)\n- `TurnResumed` — User continued speaking → cancel prepared response\n- `StartOfTurn` — User started speaking → interrupt agent if playing\n\n**Two Voice Agent Patterns:**\n1. **EndOfTurn Only** (recommended start): Simpler, fewer LLM calls, higher latency\n2. **EagerEndOfTurn + EndOfTurn**: Lower latency, more LLM calls, needs TurnResumed handling\n\n**Tuning Parameters:** `eot_threshold`, `eager_eot_threshold`, `eot_timeout_ms` — adjustable mid-stream via Configure control message.\n\n**Flux Models:** `flux-general-en` (English), `flux-general-multi` (multilingual with `language_hint`)\n\n**Relevance to OBI:** Current implementation uses Nova-3 STT via Voice Agent API (managed pipeline). Flux would require building custom STT→LLM→TTS pipeline instead of using managed Voice Agent. NOT recommended for current architecture unless latency becomes critical issue. Documented for future evaluation.\n\n### MULTI-AGENT ARCHITECTURE FINDINGS\n\nDeepgram supports orchestrating multiple specialized agents that hand off based on context/intent/domain. **Decision for OBI: Single agent with mode-switched prompts remains correct.** Multi-agent adds cold-start latency, state sync complexity, and cost overhead that outweighs benefits for our 3-mode system. {"parentUuid":"e7eafb17-2148-436d-81bd-887cc3fbfa6a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","id":"msg_01MJ2fpYac4SCnGoVyWT78rZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Thinking now. This is a good architectural question with a non-obvious answer.\n\n---\n\n## VOICE INFRASTRUCTURE EXPLORATION — TEXT ONLY\n\n### Why Deepgram is fast (the real reason)\n\nThe speed isn't just Deepgram's STT quality. It's **co-location**. STT → LLM (gpt-4o-mini managed) → TTS all run inside the same Deepgram datacenter. No cross-API hops. Each cross-API hop adds one full round-trip (200-500ms). Three hops = your whole latency budget gone before the student hears a word.\n\nAny replacement architecture has to solve this the same way: **co-locate the pipeline**.\n\n---\n\n### The actual risk you're hedging against\n\n- Deepgram tightens the \"new email = $200\" loop (email verification, card required, etc.)\n- Deepgram raises prices once you're at scale (100+ students)\n- Deepgram goes down for a day right before a demo\n- Deepgram deprecates nova-3 or gpt-4o-mini managed and forces a more expensive option\n\n---\n\n### Three architectures worth exploring\n\n---\n\n**OPTION 1 — Provider Swap Gate (1 day, no latency change)**\n\nDon't change infrastructure at all. Add a `VOICE_PROVIDER` env var to the CF Worker. The `/api/deepgram-token` endpoint returns either Deepgram credentials or Vapi/Retell credentials depending on the env var. `chat.js` adapts based on which provider is returned.\n\n- **Vapi.ai**: drop-in competitor, same managed pipeline concept (STT + LLM + TTS co-located), WebSocket protocol is different but similar enough\n- **Retell AI**: same idea, ~$0.07/min, has a free trial tier\n- **What you get**: instant failover. If Deepgram breaks, flip one env var, redeploy, students are on Vapi in 10 minutes\n- **What you don't get**: speed improvement, cost reduction\n- **Effort**: 1-2 days to wire the abstraction layer\n\n---\n\n**OPTION 2 — LiveKit Cloud, Singapore region (potentially FASTER than current)**\n\nLiveKit is the leading open-source real-time voice agent framework. They also offer a managed cloud.\n\nThe key insight: **Vietnamese students are hitting Deepgram US servers (~280ms RTT)**. LiveKit's Singapore region = ~20ms RTT from HCMC. The latency savings from geography alone could exceed any provider performance difference.\n\nArchitecture:\n```\nBrowser → LiveKit Cloud (Singapore) WebRTC/WebSocket\nLiveKit Agent → Groq Whisper STT (free 500 audio-min/day)\n             → Groq Llama-3.3-70B LLM (free 500k tokens/day)\n             → Cartesia TTS ($0.005/1K chars, first 100K chars free)\n```\n\n- **Free tier**: 50,000 minutes/month — covers current 3 students completely\n- **Cost at 100 students** (assuming 30 min/week each): ~13,000 min/month → still free tier\n- **Latency**: 20ms RTT vs 280ms = ~260ms saved per round-trip. This is significant.\n- **Provider lock-in**: zero. Swap Groq → OpenAI → Anthropic in one config line\n- **Deepgram STT can still be used** for STT quality if nova-3 matters for Vietnamese accents, just the managed LLM/TTS would be LiveKit-orchestrated\n- **Effort**: 1 week to build the LiveKit agent server + update chat.js for WebRTC\n\nThe catch: LiveKit requires a persistent server process for the agent (Python or Node SDK). That's either a Fly.io dyno (~$3/month, Singapore region) or a Hetzner VPS. Not pure serverless.\n\n---\n\n**OPTION 3 — Pure Cloudflare Stack (zero marginal cost, some latency trade-off)**\n\nFull CF-native approach. Durable Objects handle WebSocket sessions. CF Workers AI handles STT + LLM.\n\n```\nBrowser → CF Worker WebSocket → Durable Object (session state)\nDO → CF Workers AI: Whisper (STT) → Llama-3.3-70B (LLM)\n   → Cartesia or ElevenLabs (TTS, external HTTP call)\n```\n\n- **Cost**: CF Workers AI is included in CF paid plan. TTS would be the only line item.\n- **Latency risk**: CF Workers AI free tier has queue delays (1-5s). **Paid tier** (Workers AI \"Standard\") is fast. The paid plan you already have might cover this.\n- **No vendor lock-in**: entire stack is yours\n- **Problem right now**: CF Workers AI Whisper doesn't have the Vietnamese-accented English accuracy of Deepgram Nova-3. Could matter for A1 students with heavy accents.\n- **Effort**: 1-2 weeks. Significant rework of the session architecture.\n\n---\n\n### Recommendation\n\n**Now (low effort, insurance):** Wire Option 1 — the provider swap gate. One env var = instant failover. This is a 1-day task that removes the single point of failure.\n\n**When you hit ~20 students:** Pilot Option 2 (LiveKit + Singapore). The RTT savings are real and free. Run it in parallel on `/magic-chat?provider=livekit` for a few sessions and compare. If latency is equal or better, migrate fully.\n\n**If CF ever bundles fast TTS natively:** Option 3 becomes the obvious endgame — zero marginal cost, whole stack owned.\n\n---\n\n### One thing to verify before any of this\n\nThe Vietnamese-accented English accuracy of Groq Whisper vs Deepgram Nova-3. Nova-3 is specifically tuned for accented English. If Groq/Whisper accuracy drops for A1 students (more \"I don't understand\" loops), the speed gain doesn't matter. **Any new STT provider needs a real session test with Quy or a similar A1 student before committing.**\n\n---\n\n**tl;dr**: Option 1 is the insurance policy. Option 2 might actually be a speed upgrade. Option 3 is the long game. The Vietnamese geography angle (Singapore region) is underexplored and probably the highest ROI move when you're ready."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2262,"cache_read_input_tokens":84108,"output_tokens":3740,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2262,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":3,"output_tokens":3740,"cache_read_input_tokens":84108,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2262,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2262},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CbiLcPFnt6THBVYtfJy5D","type":"assistant","uuid":"9c532727-6f2e-42af-ae02-cf3477498ebf","timestamp":"2026-06-04T14:00:23.379Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"d:\\OB-1-CANONICAL","sessionId":"09888197-8173-4142-9b8e-105ac9edb415","version":"2.1.162","gitBranch":"master"}

{"parentUuid":"0364e3aa-2a2e-4716-9ed5-761f0d879224","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","id":"msg_01DBrjLUkM6HDpi9omawS6kV","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01D3pXTEdHhQyTJFLAuspMM5","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"D:\\OB-1-CANONICAL\\CANON\\RESEARCH\\voice-infrastructure-alternatives.md","content":"# CASE STUDY: Voice Infrastructure Alternatives to Deepgram\n**Filed:** 2026-06-04 | **Run:** 072 | **Status:** DEFERRED — do not implement yet\n**Tags:** #OB1 #Obi #LISTEN! #RAW #SSK\n\n---\n\n## Context\n\nCurrent architecture runs Deepgram Voice Agent as the full managed pipeline (STT + LLM + TTS) for OBI sessions with Quy, Huong, Mai Tram. Cost strategy: new email = $200 free credits each cycle. This is viable now but not at scale and not if Deepgram tightens signup controls.\n\n**Michael's directive:** Explore contingency infrastructure. Do not implement yet.\n\n---\n\n## Why Deepgram Is Fast (Root Cause)\n\nSpeed comes from **co-location**, not just model quality. STT → LLM (gpt-4o-mini managed) → TTS all run inside the same Deepgram datacenter. No cross-API hops. Each cross-API hop adds one full RTT (200-500ms). Three hops = latency budget exhausted before the student hears a word.\n\nAny replacement must solve co-location the same way.\n\n---\n\n## Risk Vectors Being Hedged\n\n1. Deepgram closes the \"new email = $200\" loop (email verification, card required)\n2. Deepgram raises prices at scale (100+ students)\n3. Deepgram outage before a live demo\n4. Deepgram deprecates nova-3 or gpt-4o-mini managed → forces expensive upgrade\n\n---\n\n## Option 1 — Provider Swap Gate (Insurance, 1 day)\n\n**#YES! — Implement first when ready. Lowest effort, highest safety.**\n\nAdd `VOICE_PROVIDER` env var to the CF Worker. `/api/deepgram-token` returns Deepgram or Vapi/Retell credentials depending on the var. `chat.js` adapts per provider.\n\n- **Vapi.ai** — managed pipeline (STT + LLM + TTS co-located), different WebSocket protocol but comparable\n- **Retell AI** — same concept, ~$0.07/min, free trial tier available\n- **Result:** Instant failover. Flip one env var, redeploy, students on Vapi in 10 minutes.\n- **Does NOT give:** speed improvement, cost reduction\n- **Effort:** 1-2 days to wire the abstraction layer\n\n---\n\n## Option 2 — LiveKit Cloud, Singapore Region (Potentially Faster)\n\n**#LISTEN! — This may be a speed UPGRADE, not just a hedge.**\n\nVietnamese students currently hit Deepgram US (~280ms RTT). LiveKit Singapore = ~20ms RTT from HCMC. The 260ms RTT savings per round-trip could exceed any provider performance difference.\n\n### Architecture\n```\nBrowser → LiveKit Cloud (Singapore) WebRTC/WebSocket\nLiveKit Agent → Groq Whisper STT (free 500 audio-min/day)\n             → Groq Llama-3.3-70B LLM (free 500k tokens/day)\n             → Cartesia TTS ($0.005/1K chars, first 100K chars free)\n```\n\n### Numbers\n| Metric | Current (Deepgram US) | Option 2 (LiveKit SGP) |\n|--------|----------------------|------------------------|\n| RTT (HCMC) | ~280ms | ~20ms |\n| STT | Nova-3 (excellent VI accent) | Groq Whisper (unverified VI accent) |\n| LLM | gpt-4o-mini managed | Groq Llama-3.3-70B (fast) |\n| TTS | Aura-2 | Cartesia (natural) |\n| Free tier | $200 credits | 50k min/month |\n| At 100 students | ~$50-100/month | Still within free tier |\n\n### Gate Before Committing\n**#RAW WARNING:** Groq Whisper accuracy on Vietnamese-accented A1 English is unverified. Nova-3 is specifically tuned for accented English. Must run a real session with Quy-level A1 student before committing to any STT provider swap. Accuracy regression > speed gain.\n\n### Infrastructure Note\nLiveKit requires a persistent agent server process (Python or Node SDK). Not pure serverless. Fly.io Singapore dyno or Hetzner VPS = ~$3-5/month.\n\n- **Effort:** 1 week. LiveKit agent build + chat.js WebRTC update.\n\n---\n\n## Option 3 — Pure Cloudflare Stack (Zero Marginal Cost, Long Game)\n\nFull CF-native. Durable Objects handle WebSocket sessions. CF Workers AI handles STT + LLM.\n\n```\nBrowser → CF Worker WebSocket → Durable Object (session state)\nDO → CF Workers AI: Whisper (STT) → Llama-3.3-70B (LLM)\n   → Cartesia or ElevenLabs (TTS, external HTTP call)\n```\n\n- **Cost:** CF Workers AI included in paid plan. TTS is the only line item.\n- **Risk:** CF Workers AI queue delays on free/standard tier. Fast only on \"Unbound\" billing.\n- **STT quality concern:** CF Whisper lacks Vietnamese-accented English tuning of Nova-3.\n- **Endgame:** If CF ever bundles fast TTS natively, this becomes zero-cost full ownership.\n- **Effort:** 1-2 weeks. Significant session architecture rework.\n\n---\n\n## Recommendation Sequence\n\n```\nNOW (1 day, insurance):\n  Option 1 — VOICE_PROVIDER swap gate\n  Removes single point of failure. Deepgram breaks → flip env var.\n\n~20 students (1 week, potential speed upgrade):\n  Option 2 pilot — LiveKit Singapore on /magic-chat?provider=livekit\n  Run parallel for a few sessions. Compare latency + accuracy.\n  If equal or better → full migration.\n\nLong game (if CF bundles fast TTS):\n  Option 3 — pure CF stack\n  Zero marginal cost, full ownership.\n```\n\n---\n\n## Critical Verification Before Any STT Swap\n\nRun a real A1 session with Quy or equivalent. Listen for:\n- Words OBI mishears that Deepgram gets right\n- Vietnamese-toned \"uh\", \"um\", false trigger rate\n- Comprehension of simplified English at 0.9x TTS speed\n\nIf Groq Whisper accuracy matches Nova-3 in Vietnamese context → Option 2 is a strict upgrade.\nIf not → stay on Deepgram STT even if switching LLM/TTS providers.\n\n---\n\n## #SSK Signals\n- VOICE_PROVIDER abstraction layer = moat (provider-agnostic architecture)\n- Singapore RTT savings = 260ms per turn = audible difference for A1 students\n- LiveKit free tier = 50k min/month = covers first 50-100 students at zero cost\n- Nova-3 VI accent accuracy = do not sacrifice for speed alone\n\n---\n*Authored by OB-1 / Agent99 — Run 072 | Do not dispatch until Michael says GO*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":588,"cache_read_input_tokens":90127,"output_tokens":2040,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":588,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2040,"cache_read_input_tokens":90127,"cache_creation_input_tokens":588,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":588},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CbiMWh25ZYz5jHXYKHgGX","type":"assistant","uuid":"170320a1-363d-4455-9bf7-433337b15747","timestamp":"2026-06-04T14:11:34.616Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"d:\\OB-1-CANONICAL","sessionId":"09888197-8173-4142-9b8e-105ac9edb415","version":"2.1.162","gitBranch":"master"}

This is the #1 session-killer on iPhone.\n\n**Blueprint:**\n\n```\nCurrent (broken on iOS):\n  onAgentDrop → stop mic tracks → setTimeout → getUserMedia() ← iOS BLOCKS THIS\n\nFix: Cache the stream instead of stopping it.\n\n  On openVoiceAgent():\n    If pendingMicStream is LIVE (readyState of all tracks === 'live'):\n      Reuse it — skip getUserMedia() entirely\n      Just reconnect the AudioWorklet/ScriptProcessor to the cached stream\n\n  On onAgentDrop():\n    DO NOT stop mic tracks\n    Just disconnect the AudioWorklet source node from the processor\n    Keep pendingMicStream alive in memory\n\n  On reconnect (agentWs reopens):\n    Reconnect AudioWorklet source to the cached pendingMicStream\n    No getUserMedia() needed — already inside iOS audio context\n\n  Safety valve (if cached stream goes stale — e.g. phone call interruption):\n    Check track.readyState === 'ended' before reuse\n    If ended → show \"Tap mic to reconnect\" button\n    That button fires getUserMedia() in the gesture handler\n    iOS allows this because it's inside a tap event\n\nTrade-off: Mic LED stays on during reconnect window (~1-3 seconds).\nAcceptable — better than dead mic.\n\nFiles: chat.js → openVoiceAgent(), onAgentDrop(), startAgentListening()\nKey variables: pendingMicStream, micAcquiring, agentWorklet\n```\n\n---\n\n### BLOCK F — Silence Timer Race Condition\n\n**Context:** Two code paths both set a silence timer that fires `InjectAgentMessage` (\"Không sao. Confusing at A1 level — sounds like OBI glitched.\n\n**Blueprint:**\n\n```\nCurrent (race condition):\n  Path 1: AgentAudioDone event → clearTimeout(silenceTimer) → setTimeout(inject, 6000)\n  Path 2: playAgentChunk queue drain → clearTimeout(silenceTimer) → setTimeout(inject, 6000)\n\n  If both paths run within the same event loop cycle:\n    Path 1 sets timer → Path 2 clears it → Path 2 sets timer\n    → ONE inject fires (correct)\n    BUT if Path 2 runs AFTER Path 1's timer already fired:\n    → TWO injects fire (bug)\n\nFix: Single source of truth.\n\n  Add: let silenceTimerOwner = null;  // 'agentAudioDone' | 'queueDrain'\n\n  Rule: Only playAgentChunk (queue drain) sets the silence timer.\n        AgentAudioDone only sets it if the audio queue is already empty.\n\n  AgentAudioDone handler:\n    if (audioQueue.length === 0 && !isPlaying) {\n      resetSilenceTimer();   // sets the 6s inject timer\n    }\n    // else: playAgentChunk drain will handle it when queue empties\n\n  playAgentChunk (after last chunk plays):\n    resetSilenceTimer();     // always the canonical setter\n\n  resetSilenceTimer():\n    clearTimeout(silenceTimer);\n    silenceTimer = setTimeout(() => { ... inject ... }, 6000);\n\nThis collapses two timer-setting paths into one canonical function.\nFiles: chat.js → AgentAudioDone handler, playAgentChunk()\n```\n\n---\n\n### BLOCK G — P2 Mobile Layout Fixes\n\n**Context:** Two small layout bugs that affect common phones. Currently `InjectionRefused` falls through the `handleAgentMsg` switch with no handler. Silent failure — OBI's silence prompt or curriculum injection simply never fires, and there's no retry.\n\n**Blueprint:**\n\n```\nAdd to handleAgentMsg switch:\n\n  case \"InjectionRefused\":\n    // Deepgram refused inject — agent not in ready state\n    // context: msg.reason (string, optional)\n    if (msg.reason) console.warn(\"[INJECT REFUSED]\", msg.reason);\n\n    // Retry strategy by inject type:\n    // - Silence prompts: retry once after 1500ms with same message\n    // - Curriculum injection (agentCurriculumInjected): clear flag, retry on next ConversationText\n    // - Vocab save confirm: drop (non-critical, already saved to KV)\n\n    if (pendingInjection) {\n      const { type, message, retryCount } = pendingInjection;\n      if (retryCount < 1) {\n        setTimeout(() => {\n          try {\n            agentWs.send(JSON.stringify({ type, behavior: \"queue\", message }));\n          } catch (_) {}\n        }, 1500);\n        pendingInjection.retryCount++;\n      } else {\n        pendingInjection = null;  // give up after 1 retry\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (!agentCurriculumInjected) {\n      // Will retry on next ConversationText trigger naturally\n    }\n\nNew variable: pendingInjection = { type, message, retryCount }\nSet pendingInjection before each silence prompt inject.\nClear on successful AgentAudioDone (inject was accepted).\n\nFiles: chat.js → handleAgentMsg, resetSilenceTimer, curriculum inject path\n```\n\n---\n\n### BLOCK J — Multiple Tab Mic Coordination\n\n**Context:** If Quy accidentally opens a second tab (common on mobile — long-press link → new tab), both tabs attempt `getUserMedia()` and both open a Deepgram WS. The second tab's WS connects last and claims the mic. First tab's mic goes silent but the WS stays \"connected\" — OBI hears nothing, responds to nothing, student thinks it's broken. No error appears.\n\n**Blueprint:**\n\n```\nUse BroadcastChannel API (supported: Chrome, Safari 15.4+, Edge — covers all target devices).\n\nOn chat.js load:\n  const obiChannel = new BroadcastChannel('obi_mic_lock');\n  const tabId = Date.now() + '_' + Math.random();\n\nOn openVoiceAgent():\n  // Claim mic ownership\n  obiChannel.postMessage({ type: 'MIC_CLAIM', tabId });\n\n  // Wait 200ms for contest\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));\n  if (micLockWinner !== tabId) {\n    // Another tab claimed it first\n    showMicStolenBanner();  // \"OBI is active in another tab — close this one\"\n    return;\n  }\n\nobiChannel.onmessage = (e) => {\n  if (e.data.type === 'MIC_CLAIM' && e.data.tabId !== tabId) {\n    if (agentWs && agentWs.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {\n      // We have the mic — contest the claim\n      obiChannel.postMessage({ type: 'MIC_OWNED', tabId });\n      micLockWinner = tabId;\n    } else {\n      // We don't have an active session — yield\n      micLockWinner = e.data.tabId;\n    }\n  }\n  if (e.data.type === 'MIC_OWNED') {\n    micLockWinner = e.data.tabId;\n  }\n};\n\nOn onAgentDrop() / page unload:\n  obiChannel.postMessage({ type: 'MIC_RELEASED', tabId });\n\nshowMicStolenBanner():\n  A banner: \"OBI is open in another tab — please close this one\"\n  Same style as networkFallbackBanner\n\nFiles: chat.js — openVoiceAgent(), onAgentDrop(), new BroadcastChannel setup\nNew variables: obiChannel, tabId, micLockWinner\n```\n\n---\n\n### BLOCK K — Corporate Firewall + Battery Saver Degradation\n\n**Context:** Two network edge cases that hit BlueTech office users specifically.\n\n**EC-13 — WSS blocked by corporate firewall:**\nCurrently, if `wss://agent.deepgram.com` is blocked at the network level, the WS fails silently or after a long timeout. The network fallback banner shows but says \"Voice busy — use text below\" — which is misleading when the real issue is a network policy.\n\n**EC-6 — Battery saver throttles KeepAlive to 10s:**\nAndroid battery saver mode throttles background timers. The 3.5s KeepAlive interval gets stretched to ~10s+. Deepgram drops idle WS connections after ~8s of silence. Deepgram audio chunks can hit this threshold during\nlong OBI responses.\n\nCurrent: ws.onmessage receives the chunk → if (e.data instanceof ArrayBuffer)\n  → accumulator logic appends to pending blob\n  \nRisk: if Safari splits a large frame, the first fragment arrives as a\n  partial ArrayBuffer that fails MP3 decode. The accumulator currently\n  checks chunk size but not whether the frame is complete.\n\nFix: Add a binary frame reassembly buffer:\n  let safariFrameBuffer = [];\n  let safariFrameExpected = -1;\n\n  ws.onmessage = (e) => {\n    if (e.data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {\n      // If small (< 64KB) → process immediately (normal path)\n      if (e.data.byteLength < 65536) {\n        processAudioChunk(e.data);\n        return;\n      }\n      // Large frame → buffer and wait for continuation\n      // NOTE: WebSocket protocol guarantees message boundaries at the\n      // protocol level; Safari bug is that it fires onmessage on each\n      // TCP segment. Reassembly: collect until we receive a frame that\n      // is NOT exactly 65536 bytes (indicates final fragment).\n      safariFrameBuffer.push(new Uint8Array(e.data));\n      if (e.data.byteLength < 65536) {\n        // Final fragment\n        const total = safariFrameBuffer.reduce((s,f) => s + f.length, 0);\n        const merged = new Uint8Array(total);\n        let offset = 0;\n        for (const f of safariFrameBuffer) { merged.set(f, offset); offset += f.length; }\n        safariFrameBuffer = [];\n        processAudioChunk(merged.buffer);\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\nFiles: public/chat.js (ws.onmessage binary handler)\nNote: This is LOW risk — only Safari desktop users are affected, and most\n  BlueTech students are on Chrome/mobile.\n```\n\n---\n\n### After These Three — DEATH\n\nRun 072 will be 12 commits covering:\n```\nA C E-H P Q-R S T-V-W X-Z AA-CC DD-FF\n```\n~35 hardening items across the full stack.
